# HVAC channel census (deliverable 1, in progress)

Built 2026-10-26 by `data/tools/census_build.py` (round 3 census-build helper) from the Videos-tab pulls of 2026-10-26 (`data/raw/census-2026-10-26/`, logged out, Latest / Popular / Oldest sorts, up to ~120 videos each). Nothing committed or pushed. **Not yet re-checked by a second helper (working rule 3).** The `type` and `sells` columns are being filled by other round 3 helpers; `type` here is carried forward from round 2 where it existed (source in the CSV, provisional where marked).

Full tables: `data/raw/census-channels-2026-10-26.csv` (every pulled channel, X included), `data/raw/census-videos-2026-10-26.csv` (every long-form video), `channels.csv` (tiers A-D).

## Method

- **Long-form** = tab duration 5:00 or more. Videos = the union of the Latest, Popular and Oldest sorts (the pull caps each at about 120). Views are the tab's **rounded** text ("24K"); exact counts come later for the top 100 and winners. Upload dates are **approximate** (pull date minus the midpoint of the age text's range, e.g. "3mo ago" = about 3.5 months before 2026-10-26).
- **HVAC title mark** (a reading by keyword rule): `is_hvac` from `data/tools/autocomplete.py` plus census fixes (word lists in `census_build.py`: `STRIP`, `CENSUS_EXCLUDE`, `DEPENDENT`/`WARMTH`, `CENSUS_STRONG`). Calibrated on the round 2 checker's 2,893 hand-marked titles: the census rule agrees on 2,786 of 2,893 (96.3%; round 2 `is_hvac` alone 2,551, 88.2%); 12 marked HVAC that the checker did not, 95 missed. This is the set the fixes were written from, so it is not an independent test (the 40-channel hand-check below is).
- **Tier rule** (`round2/check-master.md`, applied by script): D first for trade or company channels (flagged by name / About / title words, confirmed by About text; see below); A if 50%+ of the latest 30 long-form titles are HVAC; B 10-49%, C instead of B when 30%+ of those titles carry an adjacent-lane word; X under 10% (LEGACY when 30%+ of the Popular top 10 is HVAC). Fewer than 10 long-form: pooled with the Popular top 10 (LOW-N). DRIFT = an A whose newest 10 long-form hold 2 or fewer HVAC titles (flagged, kept A, per the brief for this build). Operator defaults of 2026-10-26: contractor channels teaching homeowners = A marked `contractor`; outage / off-grid channels that are mostly HVAC = A with their lane tag.
- **Lane tags**: a lane whose words appear in 3+ (and 10%+) of the latest 30 long-form titles.
- **Baseline** (labels.md section 6): median tab views of the channel's long-form uploads in the last 12 months (age text up to "11mo ago"), taken from the Latest list only; if fewer than 10, the median of its last 30 long-form. Multiple = views / baseline. Share at 2x+ is within the baseline set.
- **Uploads in 30 / 90 / 365 days** are approximate (age text); a `+` means the Latest list (about 120 uploads) ends inside that window, so the count is a floor.
- **Sub-niches**: each HVAC long-form title mapped to a `labels.md` sub-niche by keyword rule (`SUBNICHE_RULES`); top 3 by count (a reading).
- **NEW_12M**: joined on or after 2025-10-26 (About date, master CSV) or first long-form upload "11mo ago" or newer (Oldest sort). A pivot that restarted in the window is not detected here.

## The D check

D candidates are flagged by words (channel name, About text, or 30%+ trade words in the latest 30 titles) and confirmed by About-text company or trade markers (rule in `census_build.py`: `NAME_COMPANY`, `ABOUT_COMPANY`, `ABOUT_TRADE`, `TRADE`). Where the automatic reading and the round 2 tier disagreed, or a flagged company is mostly homeowner HVAC, I read the About text and titles and recorded a decision with its reason (`D_CONFIRM`, 284 channels). Contractor = a company serving local homeowners (heating-and-air companies, mechanical contractors); brands, retailers, parts sellers, trade schools and trade shows stay D even when their videos are homeowner HVAC.

## Hand-check (40 random channels, `random.Random(2610).sample` of the sorted channel IDs)

I read each sampled channel's latest 30 long-form titles (and its Popular top 10) and gave my own tier (a reading). **Agreement with the rule: 32 of 40 before fixes, 36 of 40 after** (the rule tiers below are recomputed on every run). Fixes made, all word-list misses (no threshold changed): bath / exhaust / attic fans and air-sealing words (blower door, energy audit) counted as HVAC, as `check-master.md`'s scope says; "A/C units" and "garage at 70°F" patterns; lane words "waste oil", grandma / grandpa / depression-era / old-fashioned, "cost pennies", and "7 Best ... 2026" product lists. Left as the rule has them (borderline B vs C or X, none touching tier A): Secret Sparks, NOBOX7, Halden Row (LOW-N pooling, as check-master.md found with Live Free), RomanUrsuHack. Rows: `data/raw/census-handcheck-2026-10-26.csv`.
